- **RUNBOOK**: 1. Read the rules: `ravi specs get cli/agents --mode rules --json`. 2. Reproduce the failing call with `--json` and read `error.code` first; the code, not the message, is the branch point. 3. Exit `1` + `AGENT_NOT_FOUND`: read `error.suggestions` — real agent ids and names similar to what was asked. Retry with one of them, or list with `ravi agents list --json`. 4. Exit `2`: read `error.acceptedFl ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/cli/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★6)
- **RUNBOOK**: 1. `ravi agents list --json` — list agents visible to the current principal. 2. `ravi agents show &lt;id&gt; --json` — inspect one agent, including effective model fields (`effectiveProvider`, `effectiveModel`, `modelSource`, `modelPresetId`, `modelPresetVersion`). ([filipexyz/ravi](https://github.com/filipexyz/ravi/blob/HEAD/.ravi/specs/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · ★6)
- **RUNBOOK**: The orchestration layer lives in [`packages/agent-orchestrator`](../../packages/agent-orchestrator) with a control-plane dashboard in [`packages/agents-dashboard`](../../packages/agents-dashboard). Everything is traced (Langfuse + LangSmith), uses Qdrant for long-term memory, and can file Linear issues / trigger n8n workflows / read Sentry + GitHub. The setup was guided by the official docs + refe ([aaronjullger-lgtm/PANaCEa](https://github.com/aaronjullger-lgtm/PANaCEa/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RUNBOOK.md))
- **RUNBOOK**: What broke, how it was fixed, and how to start the stack next time. ([EGAdams/planner](https://github.com/EGAdams/planner/blob/HEAD/a2a_communicating_agents/hybrid_letta_agents/agents/RUNBOOK.md) · 2 copies)
- **RUNBOOK**: Agenti jsou definovaní v `.claude/agents/&lt;name&gt;.md` → invokovatelní jako subagent (Claude Code je vidí podle `name`). Píšící agenti běží ve **vlastním git worktree off origin/master** → **PR** (= lidská brána). Plný plán + delší prompty: `~/.claude/plans/krok-0-sync-shiny-diffie.md`. ([matt03X/automationcost.io](https://github.com/matt03X/automationcost.io/blob/HEAD/scripts/agents/RUNBOOK.md))
